Let's explore mathematical optimization algorithms.

We want minimize max c_k over p simplex. This is a minimax nonconvex quadratic. Possible algorithms:
1. CEG (counterexample-guided) / SOCP: At current p, active set of convolution lags. Solve a convex problem: minimize max_{k in A} p^T Q_k p? Still nonconvex if active constraints quadratic. But if replace p^T Q_k p by linearization around p? Frank-Wolfe? The previous SOTA uses min max(f*b), linear in b, which is a kind of convex response. Could generalize: At fixed p, the best response q minimizing max (p*q) is convex. Then update p to q or blend. This is like alternating minimization of f^T? It may converge.

Can we improve by using second-order step? For objective φ(p)=max_k c_k(p), we can approximate max by softmax weights λ_k = c_k^β / sum c^β. Gradient ∇φ ≈ ∑ λ_k ∇c_k = 2∑ λ_k (Q_k p). Then use projected gradient on simplex. Previous does this with smoothing beta, Adam in log space. Could improve by:
- Use exact gradient of true max with active set rather than soft weight, or combine.
- Use larger Newton-like step? Constraint p≥0, sum=1.
- Use proximal gradient / FISTA for non-convex? Maybe adaptive line search.
- Use Barzilai-Borwein step size.
- Use line search / backtrack on objective to ensure descent.
- Use more frequent convex subproblem for active constraints (like LP) and solve more efficiently with scipy.optimize.linprog? But n large, constraints 2n-1, variables n; LP dense via convolution; can do gradient projected too.

2. Use parameterization p = σ(log w) (softmax) to handle positivity; gradient in log as above. But can also parameterize p via squared amplitudes: p_i = y_i^2 / sum y^2, y≥0 or real. Then log amplitude gradient may better? Nonnegative amplitude. But softmax okay.

- Core local optimizer for fixed n:
   We can implement a loop with multiple "modes":
   1. Cross-game mode: fixed f, update b for R inner iterations; then blend/update f.
   2. Self-gradient mode: update f via softmax gradient (soft or active) with Adam/backtrack.
   3. Multiplicative inverse-conv mode: f ← normalize(f_i / multiplier_i) with multiplier = weighted adjoint from target/current conv.
   4. Random restarts and diversification.

Let's detail algorithms.

FFT conv function:
```
def fft_convolution_full(a, N=None):
    n=a.size

Let's design a robust optimizer with a set of "operators" each producing candidate from current f:
1. `self_soft_step`: λ=softmax(c), d = 2 convcorr(λ,f) centered, natural gradient descent.

5. `symmetrize`.
6. `random_log_noise`.

def resample_to_length(p, n):
    m=len(p)
    if m==n: return normalize_nonneg(p)

       q=np.ones(n); q[0]=1? better uniform? If m=1, no good; use uniform.

    cum = np.cumsum(p); cum[0]=0? Actually invert.

We need helper functions for scores and gradients:
```
def fft_conv_array(p,N):
    F=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)
    c=c[:L?] # need L passed or len p
```
L=2n-1 can derive. Return c, maybe F.
For score: mx=max(c); if mx<0 clip; return 2*n*mx.

Convcorr λ,f:
```
def convcorr_lambda_f(lam, f, N):
    n=f.size; L=2*n-1
    lam_rev = lam[::-1]  # length L
    R = np.fft.rfft(lam_rev, N)
    F = np.fft.rfft(f, N) # can pass F if available
    h = np.fft.irfft(R*F, N)
    h = h[L-1 : L-1+n]
    h = np.maximum(h,0) # due round
    return h
```
Since λ and f nonneg, h nonnegative. If λ is not normalized? We normalize weights to sum1. d will be small.

Step helpers:
```
def make_soft_weights(c, beta):
    c = np.asarray(c)
    if c.size==0: return np.zeros(0)
    cstable = c - c.max()
    y=np.clip(beta*cstable, -745, 0) # but c may large? csum=1 so max~1e-4; beta up to maybe 1000 -> product <100. okay.
    e=np.exp(y); s=e.sum(); return e/s
```
For active top R:
```
def make_active_weights(c, r):
    if r<=0: w = zeros; w[argmax]=1
    else:
       idx=np.argpartition(-c, min(r,c.size)-1)[:r]
       w[idx]=1; w/=w.sum()
```
For inverse weights:
```
def make_inverse_weights(c, power=1):
    eps= np.finfo(float).eps * max(1, c.sum())? c sum=1; eps=1e-15.
    w=(c+eps)**(-power); normalize.
```

Let's implement Adam state for log weights:
```
def adam_update(grad_w, w, m, v, t):
   t+=1
   b1=0.9; b2=0.999
   m = b1*m+(1-b1)*grad_w
   v = b2*v+(1-b2)*grad_w*grad_w
   mhat=m/(1-b1**t)
   vhat=v/(1-b2**t)
   u=mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
   return u, m, v, t
```
Candidate for step lr: `w - lr*u`. Need clip u to avoid huge: `u=np.clip(u,-50,50)`. Try lr factors around base. Base lr maybe 0.05 to 1.0. Since u approx RMS normalized, lr ~1 gives log change O(1). We can have base_lr, factors [1,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.5? maybe 2]. For self, start lr=0.1. Accept best candidate with score<current. If found, update w and lr maybe increase; else decrease.

But m,v update should happen once per accepted or tried? We can update using grad_w before trying. If we try multiple candidates from same grad, m,v updated once. Good. If none accepted, still state updated; may hurt. We can keep it;lr reduced. If invalid, reset.

def try_self_adam(f,c,score,w,m,v,t,lr,beta):

Implement L-BFGS polish:
```
def lbfgs_polish(p, N, L, seed, time_limit):
    if no scipy: return None
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+time_limit
    best=p.copy(); best_score=fft_score(p,N)
    x0 = np.log(np.maximum(p, EPS)) - 1.0? 
    # center? x0 = log(p+eps); x0 -= x0.mean()
    betas=[5,20,80,250]
    # Optionally precompute for objective? 
    for beta in betas:
       if time>deadline: break
       # maybe run twice: one from current x, after first update.
       res = minimize(_soft_fun, x, jac=_soft_jac, args=(beta,N,L), method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':25,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':5,'disp':False,'maxfun':2000})
       if res.x finite:
          p_c=softmax(res.x)
          sc=fft_score(p_c,N)
          if sc<best_score: best=p_c; best_score=sc; x=softmax_log? update x = log(p_c+eps)-mean
       # if no improvement maybe try beta next
    return best,best_score
```
But SciPy L-BFGS with full x and function constant mean may have trouble with line search because ftol/gtol relative to c_soft ~1e-4. Set ftol=1e-15? Differences ~1e-8. Could set `gtol=1e-6`, `ftol=1e-12`. `maxfun` count includes both? Fine. It may stop early. Also line search may evaluate many; maxfun=1000 to limit. For n=5k, 1000 fun calls maybe too slow. Set maxfun=200. Each fun+grad separate (2 evals) so 400. Good. For each beta 200. 4 betas 800; maybe okay. But if N=65536, 800*4FFT ~? Could be several seconds. Accept.
Need ensure `_soft_fun` and `_soft_jac` use no global mutable state to allow parallel? no.

Maybe `scipy.optimize.minimize` with L-BFGS-B can be slow in Python if jac separate. We can pass `options={'maxiter': 15, 'ftol': 1e-10, 'gtol': 1e-7, 'maxfun': 100, 'maxls': 5}`. That's safe.

def optimize_fixed_length(p_start, n, time_budget, seed):
    if time_budget <=0: return empty
    p = resample_to_length(p_start,n)
    N=next_fast_len(3*n-2); L=2*n-1
    # quick lbfgs maybe
    best=p; best_score=fft_score
    lb_limit = min(120.0, 0.15*time_budget) if time_budget>40 else 0
    if lb_limit>0 and SCIPY_AVAILABLE:
        res=lbfgs_polish(best,N,L,lb_limit)
        if res: update best/top
    # local search with remaining deadline
    deadline = start + time_budget - 5
    # initialize states from best
    f=best; compute c/score
    reset states
    b=f copy
    lr_self=0.2; lr_cross=0.2
    stall=0; round=0
    while time<deadline:
       ...
    # final lbfgs if time >30
       res=lbfgs_polish(best, remaining 20s)
    return top,best
```

def self_adam_step(f, F, c, beta_or_mode, w, m, v, t, lr, N, L, active_r=None):

    if c is None: c, score = fft_conv...

    else: lam=soft_weights(c,beta)
    h=convcorr(lam,f,N,F) # if F precomputed
    a=2.0*h
    mean=np.dot(f,a)
    grad_w=f*(a-mean)
    maxg=np.max(np.abs(grad_w))
    if maxg <=1e-300: return None
    grad_w = grad_w / maxg? If we normalize, Adam v always ~1; then lr scale. Actually Adam already normalizes by RMS, so no need; but if large dynamic, no. We can clip grad to [-1,1]? Maybe normalize to max=1 for consistency. Let's normalize by maxg to prevent huge. Then u in Adam O(1). lr in [0.1,2]. Good.

    u,m,v,t=adam_update(grad_w,w,m,v,t)
    # try factors: [1,0.5,0.25,0.125,2,4]
    best=None
    base = lr
    for factor in (1,0.5,0.25,0.125,2,4):

        cand=exp(ww); cand/=sum

    if best_sc < current_score - 1e-12: return best (lr_new based on best_factor)

    return None

def cross_adam_step(f,F,b,w,m,v,t,lr,beta,N,L):
    B=rfft(b,N); c=irfft(F*B,N); clip; cross_sc=max(c)

`adam_update(grad, m, v, t)`:
```
beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999
m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*grad
v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*grad*grad
mhat=m/(1-beta1**t)
vhat=v/(1-beta2**t)
u=mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
u=np.where(np.isfinite(u),u,0)
u=np.clip(u,-20,20)
return u,m,v,t
```

def self_adam_step(f, F, c, mode, w, m, v, t, lr, N, L, current_score, rng?):
   lam=make_lambda(c,mode,L)
   if lam is None: return None
   h=convcorr_lambda_f(lam,f,N,F)
   a=2*h
   mean=np.dot(f,a)
   grad_w=f*(a-mean)
   maxg=np.max(np.abs(grad_w))
   if maxg <= 1e-300: return None
   grad_w /= maxg
   u,m,v,t=adam_update(grad_w,m,v,t)
   best_cand=None; best_sc=INF; best_factor=1

```
def fft_score_norm(p,N):
    n=p.size
    F=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:2*n-1]
    if c.size:
       mx=float(np.max(c))
       if not finite: return INF
       if mx<0: mx=0
       return 2*n*mx
```

L-BFGS soft functions:
```
EPS_SMALL = 1e-300

def _soft_evaluate(x, beta, N, L, want_grad):
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    if not all finite: x=np.where(finite,x,0)
    w=x - np.mean(x)
    w-=np.max(w)
    e=np.exp(w); p=e/e.sum()
    n=p.size; # L=2n-1 (could check)
    F=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:L]
    # ensure nonneg
    c=np.where(c>0,c,0)
    mx=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
    # log sum exp
    # To avoid overflow, c max ~ small, beta maybe 300, fine. But generic.
    y=beta*(c-mx)
    y=np.clip(y, -745, 0)
    e2=np.exp(y)
    s2=e2.sum()
    if s2<=0 or not finite:
       val=mx/beta? if beta=0? beta>0. Use mx
       lam=np.full(L,1/L)
    else:
       val=(mx + np.log(s2)/beta)
       lam=e2/s2
    if not want_grad: return val, None, p, c, lam, F
    h=convcorr_lambda_f(lam,p,N,F)
    a=2*h
    mean=np.dot(p,a)
    grad=p*(a-mean)
    # centered? already centered. Clip finite.
    return val, grad, p, c, lam, F

def soft_fun(x,beta,N,L): val,...=_soft_evaluate(...want_grad=False); return val
def soft_jac(x,beta,N,L): val,grad,...=_soft_evaluate(...want_grad=True); return grad
```
But calling `_soft_evaluate` separately for fun and jac recomputes. Accept.
Need if beta is 0? We use >0. If beta very small, val approximates average? okay.
Scipy minimize may pass x as ndarray float. Good.
Potential problem: `_soft_evaluate` uses `L` arg but p size may not correspond if n changed? It will be called for fixed n; okay. We can also set L=2*len(p)-1 to be safe; but args L from n. Use if `c` length differs? no.

Local loop with `f` and `F,c,score`:

Pseudo code for optimize:
```
def optimize_fixed_length(p_start, n, time_budget, seed):
    p_start=np.asarray...
    if n<16: n=256? We'll pass.
    p_start=resample_to_length(p_start,n)
    N=next_fast_len(3*n-2); L=2*n-1
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0,time_budget) - 2.0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # initial p sym choice
    p1=resample_to_length(p_start,n)
    p2=normalize_nonneg((p1+p1[::-1])/2)
    F1,c1,s1=prepare_fft(p1,N); F2,c2,s2=prepare_fft(p2,N)
    if s2 < s1: f=p2; F=F2; c=c2; score=s2; w=log_weights(f)
    else: f=p1; F=F1; c=c1; score=s1; w=...
    best=f.copy(); best_score=score
    top=[(score,best)]
    # Quick LBFGS if budget
    lb_limit = 0
    if HAVE_SCIPY and time_budget > 60:
        lb_limit = min(120.0, 0.12*time_budget)
        if lb_limit > 10:
            res=lbfgs_polish(best,N,L,lb_limit)
            if res is not None:
               p_lb, sc_lb = res
               if sc_lb < best_score - 1e-12:
                   best=p_lb; best_score=sc_lb; f=p_lb.copy(); w=log_weights(f); F,c,score=prepare_fft(f,N); top=add_top...
    # states
    m_f=zeros; v_f=zeros; t_f=0; lr_self=0.2
    b=f.copy(); w_b=log_weights(b); m_b=zeros; v_b=zeros; t_b=0; lr_cross=0.2
    beta_cross_mode=1? Cross λ use soft20 or active.
    stall=0; round=0
    have_current=True
    while time < deadline:
        round +=1
        if not have_current or c is None:
           F,c,score=prepare_fft(f,N); have_current=True
        # update best if score < best
        if score < best_score:
           best_score=score; best=f.copy(); top=add_top...
        # choose mode
        mode = round % 5  # 0 active small,1 soft5,2 soft20,3 soft80,4 active large
        # self step
        res = self_adam_step(f,F,c,mode,w,m_f,v_f,t_f,lr_self,N,L,score)
        if res is not None:
            f_new, sc_new, lr_new, w_new, m_f, v_f, t_f = res
            f=f_new; score=sc_new; w=w_new; lr_self=lr_new; F=None;c=None; have_current=False
            stall=0
        else:
            stall +=1
            lr_self=max(0.02, lr_self*0.9)
        # cross every 3 rounds or when stall >10
        if (round % 3 == 0 or stall > 10) and time < deadline:
            # ensure F,c current? no need for current; but F needed for cross
            if F is None:
                F,c,score=prepare_fft(f,N); have_current=True
            # run up to 30 cross steps
            for _ in range(30):
                if time>=deadline: break
                resb = cross_adam_step(f,F,b,w_b,m_b,v_b,t_b,lr_cross,beta_mode_cross,N,L)
                if resb is not None:
                   b=resb[1]; scb=resb[2]; lr_cross=resb[3]; w_b=resb[4]; m_b=resb[5]; v_b=resb[6]; t_b=resb[7]; cross_improved=True
                else:
                   break
            # consider blends
            # b self score maybe compute if b changed
            if time<deadline:
               best_blend=None; best_blend_sc=score
               for tval in (1.0,0.75,0.5,0.25,0.1):
                   if time>=deadline: break
                   cand = (1-tval)*f + tval*b
                   cand=normalize_nonneg(cand) # sum 1
                   sc=fft_score_norm(cand,N)
                   if sc < best_blend_sc:
                       best_blend_sc=sc; best_blend=cand
               if best_blend is not None and best_blend_sc < score - 1e-12:
                   f=best_blend; score=best_blend_sc; w=log_weights(f); m_f=zeros...; t_f=0; b=f.copy(); w_b=log_weights(b); m_b=zeros...; t_b=0; F=None;c=None; have_current=False; stall=0; lr_self=0.2; lr_cross=0.2
                   # maybe top update next loop
            else stall +=2
        # stagnation
        if stall > 40:
            # try symmetric best and noises
            if time<deadline:
               best_start=best; best_start_sc=best_score
               # sym best
               sym=normalize_nonneg((best+best[::-1])/2); sc=fft_score_norm(sym,N); if sc<best_start_sc: ...
               # random noises
               wbest=log_weights(best)
               for sig in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8,1.5):
                   wtr=wbest + rng.normal(0,sig,size=n)
                   cand=softmax_weights(wtr); sc=fft_score_norm(cand,N); if sc<best_start_sc: ...
               # set current to best_start if it is close to current or current worse
               if best_start_sc <= score + 0.02:
                   f=best_start.copy(); score=best_start_sc; w=log_weights(f); reset states; b=f.copy(); F=None;c=None
               else:
                   # move toward best_start with blend 0.5?
                   f=normalize_nonneg(0.5*f+0.5*best_start); score=fft_score_norm(f,N); reset...
               reset lr
            stall=0
        # periodic LBFGS if time budget large and round multiple 200? maybe every 200 rounds if >30s remain
        if round % 300 == 0 and time_budget > 300 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30:
             lb_limit = min(60.0, max(10.0, 0.02*time_budget))
             res=lbfgs_polish(best,N,L,lb_limit)
             if res and res[1] < best_score:
                  best=res[0]; best_score=res[1]; top=add...
                  # optionally set current to best if far
                  if best_score + 0.01 < score:
                      f=best.copy(); score=best_score; reset states; b=f.copy()
        # add top occasionally
        if round % 50 == 0 and score < INF:
             top=add_top_candidate(top,f,score)
    # final quick LBFGS if time >20
    if HAVE_SCIPY and time.monotonic() < deadline - 5 and time_budget > 120:
       lb_limit = min(deadline - time.monotonic(), 60.0)
       res=lbfgs_polish(best,N,L,lb_limit)
       if res and res[1] < best_score: update best
    # ensure top has best
    if best_score < INF: top=add_top_candidate(top,best,best_score)
    return top, best, best_score
```

```
def cross_adam_step(f,F,b,w,m,v,t,lr,mode,N,L):
    B=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*B,N)
    c=c[:L]
    if c.size:
       c=np.maximum(c,0); cross_sc=float(np.max(c))
    else: cross_sc=INF
    if not finite: return None
    lam=make_lambda(c,mode,L)
    h=convcorr_lambda_f(lam,f,N,F)
    mean=np.dot(b,h)
    grad_w=b*(h-mean)
    maxg=np.max(abs(grad_w)); if maxg<=EPS: return None
    grad_w/=maxg
    u,m,v,t=adam_update(...)
    best=None...
    for factor in CROSS_FACTORS:
       ww=w - lr*factor*u; cand=softmax; 
       # compute cross score:
       Bc=np.fft.rfft(cand,N); cc=np.fft.irfft(F*Bc,N)[:L]; cc=max(0); sc=max(cc)
       if sc<best_sc: ...
    if best_sc < cross_sc - 1e-12: return (None? cand, sc, lr_new, w_new,m,v,t)
```

next_fast_len:
```
def next_fast_len(m):
   if m<=1: return 1
   p=1
   while p<m: p <<=1
   return p
```
For N=next_fast_len(3*n-2). For n=12000, 36000 -> 65536. Good. For n=4806, 14416 -> 16384. Nice.

`normalize_nonneg`:
```
def normalize_nonneg(v):
    arr=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size==0: arr=np.ones(1)
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0.0)
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    s=float(np.sum(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
       arr=np.ones(arr.size,dtype=np.float64); s=float(arr.size)
    return arr/s
```
`enforce_floor`:
```
def enforce_floor(p):
   n=p.size
   fl=SUPPORT_FLOOR/max(1,n)
   p=np.maximum(p,fl)
   s=float(np.sum(p))
   if not finite or s==0: return ones/n
   return p/s
```
`log_weights`:
```
def log_weights(p):
   p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
   p=np.where(finite,p,0); p=np.maximum(p, EPS)
   w=np.log(p)
   w=np.where(finite,w,0); w-=float(np.max(w)); return w
```
If p has many zeros, max maybe log(EPS) for zeros or positive. Good.
`softmax_weights`:
```
def softmax_weights(w):
   w=np.asarray(w,float).ravel()
   if w.size==0: return np.ones(1)
   w=np.where(finite,w,0.0)
   mx=float(np.max(w))
   if not finite mx: mx=0
   w2=w-mx
   e=np.exp(w2)
   s=float(np.sum(e))
   if not finite or s<=0: return ones/size
   p=e/s
   # maybe no floor here
   return p
```
`fft_score_norm`:
```
def fft_score_norm(p,N):
   n=p.size
   if n==0: return INF
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)): return INF
   # if sum not exactly, formula? Use sum to be safe but slower. Could use general:
   s=float(np.sum(p))
   if not finite or s<=0: return INF
   F=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)
   L=2*n-1
   if c.size < L: maybe c=c[:c.size] else c=c[:L]
   if c.size:
      mx=float(np.max(c))
      if not finite: return INF
      if mx<0: mx=0
   else: mx=0
   return float(2*n*mx/(s*s))
```
This adds sum. For speed, maybe use assumed sum=1 except for initial? Let's use this robust; cost small.
Could avoid `np.all(finite)` for candidate (known finite). But okay. `np.all` O(n), FFT O(NlogN). fine.
`prepare_fft`:
```
def prepare_fft(p,N):
   n=p.size; L=2*n-1
   s=float(np.sum(p)); if s<=0: ...
   F=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:L]
   c=np.where(c>0,c,0)
   mx=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
   score=float(2*n*mx/(s*s)) if finite else INF
   return F,c,score
```
Assumes p normalized? formula with sum.

`convcorr_lambda_f`:
```
def convcorr_lambda_f(lam, f, N, F=None):
   n=f.size; L=2*n-1
   if F is None: F=np.fft.rfft(f,N)
   lam_rev=lam[::-1]
   R=np.fft.rfft(lam_rev,N)
   h=np.fft.irfft(R*F,N)
   start= max(0, L-1)
   h=h[start:start+n]
   if h.size<n:
      # pad
      hh=np.zeros(n); hh[:h.size]=h; h=hh
   h=np.where(np.isfinite(h),h,0.0)
   h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
   return h
```
If N maybe < start+n? We set N>=3n. good.

`soft_weights_conv`:

def make_soft_weights(c,beta):
   c=np.asarray(c,float); c=np.where(np.isfinite(c),c,0); c=np.maximum(c,0)
   if c.size==0: return np.array([])
   mx=float(np.max(c))
   if not finite or mx<=0:
      return np.full(c.size,1.0/c.size)
   y=beta*(c-mx)
   y=np.clip(y,-745.0,0.0)
   e=np.exp(y); s=float(np.sum(e))
   if not finite or s<=0: return full
   return e/s
```

def make_top_weights(c,r):
   c=np.asarray...; if c.size==0 return
   r=int(max(1,min(r,c.size)))
   w=np.zeros(c.size)
   if r>=c.size: w.fill(1)
   else:
      # use argpartition; if c has NaN? clean

      neg=-c
      if np.any(np.isnan(neg)): c=...
      idx=np.argpartition(neg, r-1)[:r]
      w[idx]=1.0
   s=w.sum(); if s>0: w/=s
   else: w.fill(1/c.size)
   return w
```

def make_lambda(c, mode, L):
   if L<=0: return np.array([])
   if mode == 0:
      r=max(1, min(8, L//1000 + 1))
      return make_top_weights(c,r)

   elif mode == 2:
      return make_soft_weights(c, 20.0)
   elif mode == 3:
      return make_soft_weights(c, 80.0)

`self_adam_step`:
```
def self_adam_step(f,F,c,mode,w,m,v,t,lr,N,L,current_score):
    lam=make_lambda(c,mode,L)
    if lam.size==0: return None
    h=convcorr_lambda_f(lam,f,N,F)
    a=2.0*h
    fl=SUPPORT_FLOOR/max(1,f.size)
    f_eff=np.maximum(f,fl)
    s_eff=float(np.sum(f_eff))
    if s_eff<=0: return None
    f_eff=f_eff/s_eff
    mean=float(np.dot(f_eff,a))
    grad_w=f_eff*(a-mean)
    grad_w=np.where(np.isfinite(grad_w),grad_w,0)
    maxg=float(np.max(np.abs(grad_w)))
    if not finite or maxg <= 1e-300: return None
    grad_w = grad_w/maxg
    u,m,v,t=adam_update(grad_w,m,v,t)
    best_cand=None; best_sc=INF; best_factor=1.0
    for fac in SELF_FACTORS:
       ww=w - (lr*fac)*u
       ww=np.where(np.isfinite(ww),ww,0.0)
       ww-=float(np.max(ww))
       cand=softmax_weights(ww)
       cand=enforce_floor(cand)
       sc=fft_score_norm(cand,N)
       if finite and sc<best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_cand=cand; best_factor=fac; best_ww=ww
    if best_cand is not None and best_sc < current_score - 1e-12:
       return (best_cand, best_sc, (lr*best_factor), best_ww, m,v,t)
    return None
```
Returning best_ww from the best factor; if multiple, store. Need define `best_ww=None`. If no improvement return None. Note m,v updated even if no accept; okay. t incremented.

```
def _soft_evaluate(x,beta,N,L,want_grad):
    x=np.asarray(x,float).ravel()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): x=np.where(np.isfinite(x),x,0)
    w=x - float(np.mean(x))
    mx=float(np.max(w)); if not finite: w=np.zeros(x.size); mx=0
    w=w-mx
    e=np.exp(w); s=e.sum(); if s<=0: p=ones/n else p=e/s
    n=p.size
    F=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:L]
    c=np.where(np.isfinite(c),c,0); c=np.maximum(c,0)
    # if L not 2n-1? maybe c longer? Use Lc=2*n-1
    Lc=2*n-1
    if c.size > Lc: c=c[:Lc]
    if c.size < Lc: c=np.pad(c,(0,Lc-c.size)? maybe not)
    mx=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
    beta=float(beta)
    if beta <= 0: val = mx? and lam uniform
    else:
       y=beta*(c-mx); y=clip(...); e2=exp(y); s2=sum
       if s2<=0: val=mx; lam=ones/Lc
       else: val=(mx + log(s2)/beta); lam=e2/s2
    if not want_grad: return val,None,p
    h=convcorr_lambda_f(lam,p,N,F)
    a=2*h
    p_eff=np.maximum(p,EPS); p_eff/=sum
    mean=dot(p_eff,a)
    grad=p_eff*(a-mean)
    grad=np.where(finite,grad,0)
    return val,grad,p
```

`lbfgs_polish`:
```
def lbfgs_polish(p, N, L, time_limit):
    if not HAVE_SCIPY or time_limit <= 1: return None
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+time_limit
    best=normalize_nonneg(p)
    best_score=fft_score_norm(best,N)
    if not finite: return None
    x=log_weights(best)
    betas=(10.0,50.0,200.0)
    # optionally run from best and sym if time
    for beta in betas:
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 5: break
       # Use current best x
       try:
          res=_sopt.minimize(soft_fun, x, jac=soft_jac, args=(beta,N,L), method="L-BFGS-B", options={"maxiter":25,"maxfun":300,"ftol":1e-12,"gtol":1e-7,"maxls":8})
       except Exception:
          break
       if hasattr(res,'x'):
          xnew=np.asarray(res.x,float).ravel()
          pc=softmax_weights(xnew)
          pc=normalize_nonneg(pc)
          sc=fft_score_norm(pc,N)
          if finite and sc<best_score-1e-12:
             best=pc; best_score=sc; x=log_weights(pc)
       # if time allows, try symmetric start for same beta? maybe not.
    return (best,best_score)
```
If no improvement, still returns best maybe same. Good. `maxfun=300` for 3 betas =900 fun; plus grad => 1800 evals. okay.

`resample_to_length`:
```
def resample_to_length(p,n):
   p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel(); p=np.maximum(finite,0); s=sum; if s<=0: p=ones(size); else p/=s
   m=p.size
   if n <=0: n=1
   if m == n: return p
   if m == 1:
       q=np.ones(n,dtype=float); return q/ n? Uniform is better; if single point, no info. return uniform.
   if n < m:
       edges=np.linspace(0,m,n+1,dtype=int)
       counts=np.diff(edges)
       sums=np.add.reduceat(p, edges[:-1])
       q=sums/np.maximum(counts,1)
   else:
       x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
       x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
       q=np.interp(x_new,x_old,p)
   q=np.where(finite,q,0); q=np.maximum(q,0); s=sum; if s<=0 q=ones/n return q/s
```
If n>m by a lot and m small, interpolation fine.

def make_length_plan(base_n):
    base_n=int(max(1024,min(base_n,MAX_N)))
    lens=[base_n]
    p2=1
    while p2<base_n: p2<<=1
    nxt=p2
    if nxt != base_n and nxt <= MAX_N: lens.append(nxt)
    twice=int(min(MAX_N, 2*base_n))
    if twice not in lens and twice > base_n: lens.append(twice)
    if MAX_N not in lens and MAX_N > max(lens): lens.append(MAX_N)
    # unique sorted maybe by len
    uniq=[]; for n in lens: if not in uniq: uniq.append(n)

    uniq.sort()
    # weights

       w=1.0

       if n == nxt: w=1.6
       if n == MAX_N and n > base_n: w=0.8
       if abs(n-8192) <= 1000: w +=0.4

    return uniq, weights

Now, let's review mathematical correctness of gradient in `_soft_evaluate`: Soft objective val = (1/β) log Σ e^{β c_k}. Derivative: λ_k = e^{β(c_k-mx)}/Σ. d val/d p_i = 2∑ λ_k p_{k-i}. We compute h=convcorr(λ,p), a=2h, grad_log = p_eff*(a - dot(p_eff,a)). Since val invariant under sum (softmax), this is gradient wrt log weights. Good.

Potential issue: In `self_adam_step`, λ from raw c (probability conv) with β. c sums 1 (for normalized f). Good. The gradient of actual max c (score/2n) is same as c; score factor constant. Good.

Potential issue: In cross step, objective min_b max c = f*b. c sums 1. λ from c. Derivative wrt b of smooth cross = h (not 2h). We use h. Good.

Potential issue: We normalize λ over L (2n-1); if sum c=1 but rounding, okay. h mean scale ~1/n.

Now, local loop with `mode = round % 5`; mode 0 active small every 5 rounds. Good. Cross mode maybe use `cross_mode = (round // 3) % 5` or same. We'll set `cross_mode = (round // 3) % 5`.

State resets: When f changes via self step accepted, we keep w_new and Adam state; good. When f changes via blend, reset m/v/t for f and b. Should we reset w_f to log f? yes.
When f changes due stall or L-BFG, reset.

Need ensure `f` is always 1D array. In blend, `normalize_nonneg` ensures. In self step candidate enforce_floor sum1. Good.

Now, exact final after L-BFG polish maybe sequence with sum=1; if exact score finite. If no exact, use FFT best. But if FFT best length maybe 12000, evaluator exact might be slow but okay.

Let's consider if returning a list of Python floats with sum1 length 12000 is okay. Yes.

Potential problem: If any returned float is `-0.0`, max(0,x) fixes. We return nonnegative.

Pseudo code for `optimize_fixed_length`:
```
def optimize_fixed_length(p_start, n, time_budget, seed):
    n=int(max(1,min(int(n),MAX_N)))
    if time_budget is None: time_budget=0
    time_budget=float(max(0.0,time_budget))
    if time_budget <= 0:
        p=resample_to_length(p_start,n)
        return [(INF,p)], p, INF
    deadline = time.monotonic()+time_budget-2.0
    if deadline <= time.monotonic(): deadline = time.monotonic()+max(1.0,time_budget)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p1=resample_to_length(p_start,n)
    p2=normalize_nonneg((p1+p1[::-1])/2.0)
    N=next_fast_len(3*n-2); L=2*n-1
    F1,c1,s1=prepare_fft(p1,N); F2,c2,s2=prepare_fft(p2,N)
    if s2 < s1: f=p2; F=F2; c=c2; score=s2
    else: f=p1; F=F1; c=c1; score=s1
    f=f.copy()
    best=f.copy(); best_score=score
    top=add_top_candidate([],best,best_score)
    w_f=log_weights(f)
    # LBFGS initial
    if HAVE_SCIPY and time_budget > 60 and time.monotonic()+10 < deadline:
        lb_limit = min(120.0, 0.12*time_budget, max(0.0, deadline-time.monotonic()-10))
        if lb_limit > 10:
            print(...)
            res=lbfgs_polish(best,N,L,lb_limit)
            if res is not None:
                p_lb, sc_lb=res
                if finite and sc_lb < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best=p_lb.copy(); best_score=sc_lb; f=best.copy(); score=best_score; F,c,_=prepare_fft(f,N); w_f=log_weights(f)
                    top=add_top_candidate(top,best,best_score)
    # states
    m_f=np.zeros(n); v_f=np.zeros(n); t_f=0; lr_self=0.2
    b=f.copy(); w_b=log_weights(b); m_b=np.zeros(n); v_b=np.zeros(n); t_b=0; lr_cross=0.2
    stall=0; round=0; last_print=time.monotonic()
    have_current=True
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        round +=1
        if not have_current:
            F,c,score=prepare_fft(f,N); have_current=True
        if score < best_score:
            best_score=score; best=f.copy(); top=add_top_candidate(top,best,best_score)
        mode=round % 5
        res=self_adam_step(f,F,c,mode,w_f,m_f,v_f,t_f,lr_self,N,L,score)
        if res is not None:
            f_new,sc_new,lr_new,w_new,m_f,v_f,t_f=res
            f=f_new; score=sc_new; lr_self=lr_new; w_f=w_new; have_current=False
            stall=0
        else:
            stall+=1
            lr_self=max(0.02, lr_self*0.9)
        if (round % 3 == 0 or stall > 8) and time.monotonic() < deadline:
            if F is None or not have_current:
                F,c,score=prepare_fft(f,N); have_current=True
            cross_mode=(round//3)%5
            did_cross=False
            for _ in range(12):
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
                resb=cross_adam_step(f,F,b,w_b,m_b,v_b,t_b,lr_cross,cross_mode,N,L)
                if resb is not None:
                    b_new,scb,lr_new,w_new,m_b,v_b,t_b=resb
                    b=b_new; lr_cross=lr_new; w_b=w_new; did_cross=True
                else:
                    break
            # blend attempt
            if time.monotonic() < deadline:
                best_blend=None; best_blend_sc=score
                for tval in BLEND_TS:
                    if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
                    cand=(1.0-tval)*f + tval*b
                    cand=normalize_nonneg(cand)
                    sc=fft_score_norm(cand,N)
                    if finite and sc < best_blend_sc:
                        best_blend_sc=sc; best_blend=cand
                if best_blend is not None and best_blend_sc < score - 1e-12:
                    f=best_blend; score=best_blend_sc
                    w_f=log_weights(f); m_f=np.zeros(n); v_f=np.zeros(n); t_f=0
                    b=f.copy(); w_b=log_weights(b); m_b=np.zeros(n); v_b=np.zeros(n); t_b=0
                    lr_self=0.2; lr_cross=0.2
                    have_current=False; stall=0
            else:
                stall +=2
                lr_cross=max(0.02, lr_cross*0.9)
        # LBFGS periodic
        if round % 400 == 0 and time_budget > 300 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30:
            lb_limit = min(60.0, 0.02*time_budget, max(0.0, deadline-time.monotonic()-30))
            if lb_limit > 20:
                res=lbfgs_polish(best,N,L,lb_limit)
                if res:
                   p_lb, sc_lb=res
                   if finite and sc_lb < best_score:
                       best=p_lb.copy(); best_score=sc_lb; top=add...
                       if best_score + 0.005 < score:
                           f=best.copy(); score=best_score; reset states; have_current=False
        # stagnation
        if stall > 40:
            if time.monotonic() < deadline:
                best_start=best; best_start_sc=best_score
                sym=normalize_nonneg((best+best[::-1])/2.0)
                sc=fft_score_norm(sym,N)
                if finite and sc < best_start_sc:
                    best_start=sym; best_start_sc=sc
                wbest=log_weights(best)
                for sig in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8,1.5):
                    if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
                    wtr=wbest + rng.normal(0.0, sig, size=n)
                    cand=softmax_weights(wtr)
                    cand=enforce_floor(cand)
                    sc=fft_score_norm(cand,N)
                    if finite and sc < best_start_sc:
                        best_start=cand; best_start_sc=sc
                # choose current
                if best_start_sc <= score + 0.02:
                    f=best_start.copy(); score=best_start_sc
                else:
                    f=normalize_nonneg(0.5*f + 0.5*best_start)
                    score=fft_score_norm(f,N)
                w_f=log_weights(f); m_f=zeros...; b=f.copy(); w_b=...; have_current=False; lr reset; stall=0
            else:
                stall=0
        if time.monotonic() - last_print > 120:
            print(f"  len {n} current {score:.6f} best {best_score:.6f} stall {stall}")
            last_print=time.monotonic()
    # final lbfgs
    if HAVE_SCIPY and time_budget > 120 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       lb_limit=min(60.0, max(0,rem-5))
       if lb_limit >10:
          res=lbfgs_polish(best,N,L,lb_limit)
          if res and ... update best/top
    if np.isfinite(best_score): top=add_top_candidate(top,best,best_score)
    return top, best, best_score
```

def safe_final_sequence(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   if a.size==0: a=np.ones(1024)
   a=np.where(finite,a,0); a=np.maximum(a,0)
   n=a.size
   s=sum
   if not finite or s<=0: a=ones/n; s=n
   # scale to sum 1.0 (or keep if sum>0.01). To be safe, sum 1.
   a /= s
   # If any value >1000? max initially maybe if sum scaled? If a had huge values, dividing sum1 makes max<=1. Good.
   # Ensure sum >=0.01.
   s2=sum; if s2<0.01: a*=0.01/max(s2,eps)
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   return [float(x) for x in a]
```

```
def fft_score_norm(p,N):
   n=p.size
   s=float(np.sum(p))
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=0: return INF
   F=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:2*n-1]
   if c.size:
      mx=float(np.max(c))
      if not np.isfinite(mx): return INF
      if mx<0: mx=0.0
   else: mx=0.0
   return float(2.0*n*mx/(s*s))
```
Good. In `prepare_fft`, same.

Now, we should maybe set `options={'maxiter': 20, 'maxfun': 300, 'ftol': 1e-12, 'gtol': 1e-6, 'maxls': 5}`. `gtol` small maybe stops slowly? But maxiter/fun caps. Use 1e-6.

Now, if `scipy` import takes time? negligible.

Now, final code. Ensure no line too long? okay.

Let's now produce final code. We'll include comments minimal.

Double-check exact code for `lbfgs_polish`: In final call after exact, if rem > 45, lb_limit = min(30, rem-20). Good. But if exact validation not timed, `time.monotonic()` may have advanced. okay.

Potential issue: `exact_score_normalized` on a list from safe final not yet list; accepts array. Good.

Potential issue: In main exact validation, `candidates.sort(key=_score_key)` where `_score_key` expects item. If candidates include None? We only append if global_best not None. In fallback, global_best maybe parent. Good.

Potential issue: In `load_initial_sequence`, downsampling to MAX_N uses `blocks = int(arr.size) // target`; if arr.size=13000,target=12000, blocks=1, else arr=arr[:target]. Good. But if arr.size=25000,target=12000, blocks=2, use=24000, reshape target,2 average -> length 12000, discards 1000. okay.

Potential issue: In `resample_to_length`, if p is already normalized length maybe 1, returns uniform. If n small? okay.

Potential issue: In `optimize_fixed_length`, after `lbfgs_polish` initial, if `lb_limit` computed with `deadline - time.monotonic() - 10` could be negative; `max(0.0, ...)`. Good.

Potential issue: In local loop, after self step accepted, `F` still old but `have_current=False`; next top compute. But if cross phase immediately in same round (round%3) and `have_current=False`, it will recompute F/c before cross. Good.

Potential issue: In local loop, after self step accepted, `b` not reset. If f changed significantly, b stale. But cross phase may use stale b. That's okay, it adapts. If self improved many rounds, b may be far; blend with b maybe bad but line search by score only accepts if improves. Cross step updates b to new f over iterations.

Potential issue: In cross phase, `b` initially f. After several rounds, if f changes by small self steps, b maybe updated. Good.

Potential issue: In `_soft_evaluate`, if `x` length n and p after softmax sum1. If n large and x mean large, `w=x-mean` might be huge positive/negative? Softmax subtract max. okay.

if grad is None: return np.zeros_like(x)
grad=np.where(np.isfinite(grad),grad,0)
return grad

Good.

Potential issue: In `_soft_evaluate`, `p_eff = np.maximum(p, EPS); p_eff /= sum` used for gradient; if p has zeros, gradient zero. okay.

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative sequences minimizing
    2 * n * max(np.convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2
by working in the scale-invariant normalization sum(a)=1.

The optimizer combines:
  - smooth max-convolution gradient descent in log-simplex space,
  - an alternating convex "best response" subproblem min_b max(f*b),
  - L-BFGS polishing of a log-sum-exp relaxation,
  - multi-length restarts, symmetrization, structured random restarts,
  - final validation with exact np.convolve.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    import scipy.optimize as _sopt
    HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY = False

EPS = 1e-300
INF = float("inf")
MAX_N = 12000
SUPPORT_FLOOR = 1e-12
SELF_FACTORS = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 2.0)
CROSS_FACTORS = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 2.0)
BLEND_TS = (1.0, 0.75, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1)


def next_fast_len(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def next_power_ge(x):
    return next_fast_len(int(x))


def normalize_nonneg(v):
    arr = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        arr = np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def enforce_floor(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    fl = SUPPORT_FLOOR / float(max(1, n))
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, fl)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return p / s


def log_weights(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, EPS)
    w = np.log(p)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    w -= float(np.max(w))
    return w


def softmax_weights(w):
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = w.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(w))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        mx = 0.0
    w = w - mx
    e = np.exp(w)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return e / s


def fft_score_norm(p, N):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return INF
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return INF
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, N)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if c.size <= L:
        c = c[: c.size]
    else:
        c = c[: L]
    if c.size:
        mx = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(mx):
            return INF
        if mx < 0.0:
            mx = 0.0
    else:
        mx = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))


def prepare_fft(p, N):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        F = np.zeros(N // 2 + 1, dtype=np.complex128)
        c = np.zeros(max(0, L), dtype=np.float64)
        return F, c, INF
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, N)[:L]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    if c.size:
        mx = float(np.max(c))
    else:
        mx = 0.0
    score = float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s)) if np.isfinite(mx) else INF
    return F, c, score


def convcorr_lambda_f(lam, f, N, F=None):
    n = f.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if F is None:
        F = np.fft.rfft(f, N)
    lam = np.asarray(lam, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    lam = np.where(np.isfinite(lam), lam, 0.0)
    lam_rev = lam[::-1]
    R = np.fft.rfft(lam_rev, N)
    h = np.fft.irfft(R * F, N)
    start = max(0, L - 1)
    h = h[start:start + n]
    if h.size < n:
        hh = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        hh[: h.size] = h
        h = hh
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
    return h


def make_soft_weights(c, beta):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
        return np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
    y = float(beta) * (c - mx)
    y = np.clip(y, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def make_top_weights(c, r):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = c.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    r = int(max(1, min(int(r), n)))
    if r >= n:
        w = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        w = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        neg = -c
        idx = np.argpartition(neg, r - 1)[:r]
        w[idx] = 1.0
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        w = w / s
    return w


def make_lambda(c, mode, L):
    if L <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if mode == 0:
        r = max(1, min(8, int(L // 1000) + 1))
        return make_top_weights(c, r)
    if mode == 1:
        return make_soft_weights(c, 5.0)
    if mode == 2:
        return make_soft_weights(c, 20.0)
    if mode == 3:
        return make_soft_weights(c, 80.0)
    r = max(8, int(L // 100))
    return make_top_weights(c, r)


def adam_update(grad_w, m, v, t):
    t = int(t) + 1
    b1 = 0.9
    b2 = 0.999
    grad_w = np.asarray(grad_w, dtype=np.float64)
    m = m * b1 + (1.0 - b1) * grad_w
    v = v * b2 + (1.0 - b2) * (grad_w * grad_w)
    mhat = m / (1.0 - b1 ** t)
    vhat = v / (1.0 - b2 ** t)
    u = mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-8)
    u = np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, 0.0)
    u = np.clip(u, -20.0, 20.0)
    return u, m, v, t


def self_adam_step(f, F, c, mode, w, m, v, t, lr, N, L, current_score):
    lam = make_lambda(c, mode, L)
    if lam.size == 0:
        return (False, None, None, None, None, m, v, t)

    h = convcorr_lambda_f(lam, f, N, F)
    a = 2.0 * h
    fl = SUPPORT_FLOOR / float(max(1, f.size))
    fe = np.maximum(f, fl)
    se = float(np.sum(fe))
    if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 0.0:
        return (False, None, None, None, None, m, v, t)
    fe = fe / se

    mean = float(np.dot(fe, a))
    grad_w = fe * (a - mean)
    grad_w = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_w), grad_w, 0.0)
    maxg = float(np.max(np.abs(grad_w)))
    if not np.isfinite(maxg) or maxg <= 1e-300:
        return (False, None, None, None, None, m, v, t)
    grad_w = grad_w / maxg

    u, m, v, t = adam_update(grad_w, m, v, t)

    best_cand = None
    best_sc = INF
    best_fact = 1.0
    best_ww = None

    for fac in SELF_FACTORS:
        step = float(lr * fac)
        ww = w - step * u
        ww = np.where(np.isfinite(ww), ww, 0.0)
        ww -= float(np.max(ww))
        cand = softmax_weights(ww)
        cand = enforce_floor(cand)
        sc = fft_score_norm(cand, N)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_cand = cand
            best_fact = fac
            best_ww = ww

    if best_cand is not None and best_sc < current_score - 1e-12:
        return (True, best_cand, float(best_sc), float(lr * best_fact), best_ww, m, v, t)

    return (False, None, None, None, None, m, v, t)


def cross_adam_step(f, F, b, w, m, v, t, lr, mode, N, L):
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * B, N)[:L]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    if c.size:
        cross_sc = float(np.max(c))
    else:
        cross_sc = 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(cross_sc):
        return (False, None, None, None, None, m, v, t)

    lam = make_lambda(c, mode, L)
    if lam.size == 0:
        return (False, None, None, None, None, m, v, t)

    h = convcorr_lambda_f(lam, f, N, F)
    fl = SUPPORT_FLOOR / float(max(1, b.size))
    be = np.maximum(b, fl)
    sb = float(np.sum(be))
    if not np.isfinite(sb) or sb <= 0.0:
        return (False, None, None, None, None, m, v, t)
    be = be / sb

    mean = float(np.dot(be, h))
    grad_w = be * (h - mean)
    grad_w = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_w), grad_w, 0.0)
    maxg = float(np.max(np.abs(grad_w)))
    if not np.isfinite(maxg) or maxg <= 1e-300:
        return (False, None, None, None, None, m, v, t)
    grad_w = grad_w / maxg

    u, m, v, t = adam_update(grad_w, m, v, t)

    best_cand = None
    best_sc = INF
    best_fact = 1.0
    best_ww = None

    for fac in CROSS_FACTORS:
        step = float(lr * fac)
        ww = w - step * u
        ww = np.where(np.isfinite(ww), ww, 0.0)
        ww -= float(np.max(ww))
        cand = softmax_weights(ww)
        cand = enforce_floor(cand)
        Bc = np.fft.rfft(cand, N)
        cc = np.fft.irfft(F * Bc, N)[:L]
        cc = np.where(np.isfinite(cc), cc, 0.0)
        cc = np.maximum(cc, 0.0)
        sc = float(np.max(cc)) if cc.size else 0.0
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_cand = cand
            best_fact = fac
            best_ww = ww

    if best_cand is not None and best_sc < cross_sc - 1e-12:
        return (True, best_cand, float(best_sc), float(lr * best_fact), best_ww, m, v, t)

    return (False, None, None, None, None, m, v, t)


def _soft_evaluate(x, beta, N, L, want_grad):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    n = x.size
    w = x - float(np.mean(x))
    mx = float(np.max(w))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        w = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        mx = 0.0
    w = w - mx
    e = np.exp(w)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        p = e / s

    F = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, N)[:L]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    if c.size:
        cmx = float(np.max(c))
    else:
        cmx = 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(cmx):
        cmx = 0.0

    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 0.0:
        val = cmx
        lam = np.full(max(1, L), 1.0 / float(max(1, L)), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        y = beta * (c - cmx)
        y = np.clip(y, -745.0, 0.0)
        e2 = np.exp(y)
        s2 = float(np.sum(e2))
        if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 0.0:
            val = cmx
            lam = np.full(max(1, L), 1.0 / float(max(1, L)), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            val = float(cmx + np.log(s2) / beta)
            lam = e2 / s2

    if not want_grad:
        return val, None, p

    h = convcorr_lambda_f(lam, p, N, F)
    a = 2.0 * h
    pe = np.maximum(p, EPS)
    spe = float(np.sum(pe))
    if not np.isfinite(spe) or spe <= 0.0:
        pe = np.maximum(p, 1.0)
        spe = float(np.sum(pe))
    pe = pe / spe
    mean = float(np.dot(pe, a))
    grad = pe * (a - mean)
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return val, grad, p


def soft_fun(x, beta, N, L):
    val, _, _ = _soft_evaluate(x, beta, N, L, False)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e100
    return float(val)


def soft_jac(x, beta, N, L):
    _, grad, _ = _soft_evaluate(x, beta, N, L, True)
    if grad is None:
        return np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return grad


def lbfgs_polish(p, N, L, time_limit, betas=(10.0, 50.0, 200.0)):
    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
        return None
    time_limit = float(max(0.0, time_limit))
    if time_limit <= 1.0:
        return None
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + time_limit

    best = normalize_nonneg(p)
    best_score = fft_score_norm(best, N)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return None

    x = log_weights(best)

    for beta in betas:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 5.0:
            break
        try:
            res = _sopt.minimize(
                soft_fun,
                x,
                jac=soft_jac,
                args=(float(beta), N, L),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "maxiter": 25,
                    "maxfun": 300,
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "gtol": 1e-6,
                    "maxls": 8,
                    "disp": False,
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            xnew = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            pc = softmax_weights(xnew)
            pc = normalize_nonneg(pc)
            sc = fft_score_norm(pc, N)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best = pc
                best_score = sc
                x = log_weights(pc)

    return best, best_score


def exact_score_normalized(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return INF
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return INF
    b = np.convolve(a, a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return INF
    mx = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return INF
    return float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))


def resample_to_length(p, n):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(max(1, n))
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    p = p / s
    m = p.size

    if m == n:
        return p
    if m == 1:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    if n < m:
        edges = np.linspace(0, m, n + 1, dtype=int)
        counts = np.diff(edges)
        sums = np.add.reduceat(p, edges[:-1])
        q = sums / np.maximum(counts, 1)
    else:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        q = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)

    q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(q))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return q / s


def length_priority(n):
    p = 0.0
    if n == 8192:
        p += 1000.0
    elif 6000 <= n <= 11000:
        p += 400.0
    elif 3000 <= n <= 5000:
        p += 200.0
    if n > 0 and (n & (n - 1)) == 0:
        p += 300.0
    p -= 0.03 * max(0, n - 10000)
    return p


def make_length_plan(base_n):
    base_n = int(max(1024, min(int(base_n), MAX_N)))
    raw = [base_n]
    p = next_power_ge(base_n)
    for mult in (1, 2, 4, 8, 16):
        cand = p * mult
        if cand > base_n and cand <= MAX_N and cand not in raw:
            raw.append(cand)
    if MAX_N > base_n and MAX_N not in raw:
        raw.append(MAX_N)

    unique = []
    for x in raw:
        if x not in unique:
            unique.append(x)

    others = [x for x in unique if x != base_n]
    others.sort(key=length_priority, reverse=True)
    chosen = [base_n] + others[:2]

    chosen = []
    for x in [base_n] + others[:2]:
        if x not in chosen:
            chosen.append(x)

    chosen.sort()

    weights = []
    for n in chosen:
        w = 1.0
        if n == 8192:
            w = 1.8
        elif n >= 10000:
            w = 1.0
        else:
            w = 0.9
        weights.append(w)

    return chosen, weights


def add_top_candidate(top, a, score, max_items=10):
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return top
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if len(top) < max_items:
        top.append((float(score), a))
        return top

    worst_idx = 0
    worst_score = float(top[0][0])
    for i in range(1, len(top)):
        si = float(top[i][0])
        if si > worst_score:
            worst_score = si
            worst_idx = i

    if float(score) < worst_score:
        top[worst_idx] = (float(score), a)
    return top


def load_initial_sequence():
    obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if obj is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(obj, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size < 1:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None

    if arr.size > MAX_N:
        target = MAX_N
        blocks = int(arr.size) // target
        if blocks > 1:
            use = target * blocks
            arr = np.mean(arr[:use].reshape(target, blocks), axis=1)
        else:
            arr = arr[:target]
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
        s = float(np.sum(arr))
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            arr = np.ones(MAX_N, dtype=np.float64)
            s = float(MAX_N)

    return arr / s


def optimize_fixed_length(p_start, n, time_budget, seed):
    n = int(max(1, min(int(n), MAX_N)))
    time_budget = float(max(0.0, time_budget))
    if time_budget <= 0.0:
        p = resample_to_length(p_start, n)
        return [(INF, p)], p, INF

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, time_budget - 2.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    p1 = resample_to_length(p_start, n)
    p2 = normalize_nonneg((p1 + p1[::-1]) / 2.0)
    N = next_fast_len(3 * n - 2)
    L = 2 * n - 1

    F1, c1, s1 = prepare_fft(p1, N)
    F2, c2, s2 = prepare_fft(p2, N)

    if s2 < s1:
        f = p2.copy()
        F = F2
        c = c2
        score = s2
    else:
        f = p1.copy()
        F = F1
        c = c1
        score = s1

    best = f.copy()
    best_score = score
    top = add_top_candidate([], best, best_score)
    w_f = log_weights(f)

    if HAVE_SCIPY and time_budget > 60.0 and time.monotonic() + 12.0 < deadline:
        lb_limit = min(120.0, 0.12 * time_budget, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 12.0))
        if lb_limit > 10.0:
            res = lbfgs_polish(best, N, L, lb_limit)
            if res is not None:
                plb, slb = res
                if np.isfinite(slb) and slb < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best = plb.copy()
                    best_score = slb
                    f = best.copy()
                    score = best_score
                    F, c, _ = prepare_fft(f, N)
                    w_f = log_weights(f)
                    top = add_top_candidate(top, best, best_score)

    m_f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v_f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t_f = 0
    lr_self = 0.2

    b = f.copy()
    w_b = log_weights(b)
    m_b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v_b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t_b = 0
    lr_cross = 0.2

    stall = 0
    outer = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()
    have_current = True

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        outer += 1

        if not have_current:
            F, c, score = prepare_fft(f, N)
            have_current = True

        if score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best = f.copy()
            top = add_top_candidate(top, best, best_score)

        mode = outer % 5
        ok, f_new, sc_new, lr_new, w_new, m_f, v_f, t_f = self_adam_step(
            f, F, c, mode, w_f, m_f, v_f, t_f, lr_self, N, L, score
        )
        if ok:
            f = f_new
            score = sc_new
            lr_self = lr_new
            w_f = w_new
            have_current = False
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            lr_self = max(0.02, lr_self * 0.85)

        if (outer % 3 == 0 or stall > 8) and time.monotonic() < deadline:
            if not have_current:
                F, c, score = prepare_fft(f, N)
                have_current = True

            for j in range(12):
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    break
                cm = ((outer // 3) + j) % 5
                okb, b_new, scb, lrb, wb_new, m_b, v_b, t_b = cross_adam_step(
                    f, F, b, w_b, m_b, v_b, t_b, lr_cross, cm, N, L
                )
                if okb:
                    b = b_new
                    lr_cross = lrb
                    w_b = wb_new
                else:
                    lr_cross = max(0.02, lr_cross * 0.95)
                    break

            if time.monotonic() < deadline:
                best_blend = None
                best_blend_sc = score
                for tv in BLEND_TS:
                    if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                        break
                    cand = (1.0 - tv) * f + tv * b
                    cand = normalize_nonneg(cand)
                    sc = fft_score_norm(cand, N)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_blend_sc:
                        best_blend_sc = sc
                        best_blend = cand
                if best_blend is not None and best_blend_sc < score - 1e-12:
                    f = best_blend
                    score = best_blend_sc
                    w_f = log_weights(f)
                    m_f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    v_f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    t_f = 0
                    b = f.copy()
                    w_b = log_weights(b)
                    m_b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    v_b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    t_b = 0
                    lr_self = 0.2
                    lr_cross = 0.2
                    have_current = False
                    stall = 0
                else:
                    stall += 2

        if outer % 400 == 0 and time_budget > 300.0 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
            lb_limit = min(60.0, 0.02 * time_budget, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 30.0))
            if lb_limit > 20.0 and HAVE_SCIPY:
                res = lbfgs_polish(best, N, L, lb_limit)
                if res is not None:
                    plb, slb = res
                    if np.isfinite(slb) and slb < best_score:
                        best = plb.copy()
                        best_score = slb
                        top = add_top_candidate(top, best, best_score)
                        if best_score + 0.005 < score:
                            f = best.copy()
                            score = best_score
                            w_f = log_weights(f)
                            m_f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                            v_f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                            t_f = 0
                            b = f.copy()
                            w_b = log_weights(b)
                            m_b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                            v_b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                            t_b = 0
                            have_current = False

        if stall > 40 and time.monotonic() < deadline:
            best_start = best
            best_start_sc = best_score

            sym = normalize_nonneg((best + best[::-1]) / 2.0)
            sc = fft_score_norm(sym, N)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_start_sc:
                best_start = sym
                best_start_sc = sc

            wbest = log_weights(best)
            for sig in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.8, 1.5):
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    break
                wtr = wbest + rng.normal(0.0, float(sig), size=n)
                cand = softmax_weights(wtr)
                cand = enforce_floor(cand)
                sc = fft_score_norm(cand, N)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_start_sc:
                    best_start = cand
                    best_start_sc = sc

            if best_start_sc <= score + 0.02:
                f = best_start.copy()
                score = best_start_sc
            else:
                f = normalize_nonneg(0.5 * f + 0.5 * best_start)
                score = fft_score_norm(f, N)

            w_f = log_weights(f)
            m_f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            v_f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            t_f = 0
            b = f.copy()
            w_b = log_weights(b)
            m_b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            v_b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            t_b = 0
            lr_self = 0.2
            lr_cross = 0.2
            have_current = False
            stall = 0

        if stall > 140 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
            idx_arr = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
            best_global = best.copy()
            best_global_sc = best_score

            uni = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
            sc = fft_score_norm(uni, N)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_global_sc:
                best_global = uni
                best_global_sc = sc

            rnorm = np.abs(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n))
            rnorm = normalize_nonneg(rnorm)
            sc = fft_score_norm(rnorm, N)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_global_sc:
                best_global = rnorm
                best_global_sc = sc

            for _ in range(6):
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 60.0:
                    break
                lo = 2
                hi = max(3, int(n // 4) + 1)
                freq = int(rng.integers(lo, hi))
                amp = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.9))
                ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
                cc = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * idx_arr / float(n) + ph)
                cc = np.maximum(cc, 0.0)
                cand = normalize_nonneg(cc)
                sc = fft_score_norm(cand, N)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_global_sc:
                    best_global = cand
                    best_global_sc = sc

            if np.isfinite(best_global_sc) and best_global_sc < best_score + 0.1:
                f = best_global.copy()
                score = best_global_sc
                if score < best_score:
                    best = f.copy()
                    best_score = score
                    top = add_top_candidate(top, best, best_score)
            else:
                f = best.copy()
                score = best_score

            w_f = log_weights(f)
            m_f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            v_f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            t_f = 0
            b = f.copy()
            w_b = log_weights(b)
            m_b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            v_b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            t_b = 0
            have_current = False
            stall = 0

        if time.monotonic() - last_print > 120.0:
            print(f"  len {n} current {score:.6f} best {best_score:.6f} stall {stall}")
            last_print = time.monotonic()

    if HAVE_SCIPY and time_budget > 120.0 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        lb_limit = min(60.0, max(0.0, rem - 5.0))
        if lb_limit > 10.0:
            res = lbfgs_polish(best, N, L, lb_limit)
            if res is not None:
                plb, slb = res
                if np.isfinite(slb) and slb < best_score:
                    best = plb.copy()
                    best_score = slb
                    top = add_top_candidate(top, best, best_score)

    if np.isfinite(best_score):
        top = add_top_candidate(top, best, best_score)

    return top, best, best_score


def safe_final_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n = a.size
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n)
    a = a / s
    s2 = float(np.sum(a))
    if s2 < 0.01:
        a = a * (0.01 / max(s2, EPS))
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in a]


def _score_key(item):
    return item[0]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    budget = min(max(0.0, budget), 2385.0)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    final_deadline = t0 + budget - 1.0

    parent = load_initial_sequence()
    if parent is None or parent.size < 16:
        parent = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64) / 4096.0
    parent = normalize_nonneg(parent)

    lengths, weights = make_length_plan(parent.size)
    print(f"initial parent length = {parent.size}")
    print(f"planned lengths = {lengths}, weights = {weights}")

    search_reserve = min(60.0, max(10.0, 0.05 * budget))
    search_deadline = t0 + budget - search_reserve
    if search_deadline < t0 + 30.0:
        search_deadline = final_deadline - 15.0

    current_seed = parent
    global_best = None
    global_best_score = INF
    all_top = []

    for i, n_len in enumerate(lengths):
        if time.monotonic() >= search_deadline - 5.0:
            break

        rem_search = max(0.0, search_deadline - time.monotonic())
        rem_weight = 0.0
        for j in range(i, len(weights)):
            rem_weight += float(weights[j])
        if rem_weight <= 0.0 or rem_search <= 0.0:
            break

        alloc = rem_search * (float(weights[i]) / rem_weight)
        alloc = min(rem_search, max(20.0, alloc))

        top, best_arr, best_sc = optimize_fixed_length(
            current_seed, n_len, alloc, int(seed) + 101 * i
        )
        all_top.extend(top)
        if np.isfinite(best_sc) and best_sc < global_best_score:
            global_best_score = best_sc
            global_best = best_arr.copy()
            current_seed = best_arr.copy()
        print(f"length {n_len} best FFT score = {best_sc:.6f}")

    if global_best is None:
        global_best = normalize_nonneg(parent)
        N = next_fast_len(3 * global_best.size - 2)
        global_best_score = fft_score_norm(global_best, N)

    candidates = list(all_top)
    candidates.append((global_best_score, global_best))
    if candidates:
        candidates.sort(key=_score_key)

    best_exact_seq = None
    best_exact_score = INF
    exact_checks = 0

    for fft_sc, arr in candidates:
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        n = arr.size
        if n <= 1:
            continue
        if np.isfinite(best_exact_score) and float(fft_sc) > best_exact_score + 1e-4:
            break
        if time.monotonic() > final_deadline - 25.0:
            break
        if n > MAX_N:
            continue

        es = exact_score_normalized(arr)
        exact_checks += 1
        if np.isfinite(es) and es < best_exact_score:
            best_exact_score = es
            best_exact_seq = arr.copy()

        if exact_checks >= 10:
            break

    if best_exact_seq is None:
        best_exact_seq = np.asarray(global_best, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if not best_exact_seq.size:
            best_exact_seq = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
        if np.isfinite(global_best_score):
            best_exact_score = global_best_score

    if (
        HAVE_SCIPY
        and best_exact_seq is not None
        and 16 <= len(best_exact_seq) <= MAX_N
        and time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 45.0
    ):
        nfin = len(best_exact_seq)
        Nfin = next_fast_len(3 * nfin - 2)
        Lfin = 2 * nfin - 1
        rem = max(0.0, final_deadline - time.monotonic())
        limit = min(30.0, rem - 20.0)
        if limit > 10.0:
            res = lbfgs_polish(best_exact_seq, Nfin, Lfin, limit, betas=(20.0, 100.0, 400.0))
            if res is not None:
                pfinal, _ = res
                if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 20.0:
                    es = exact_score_normalized(pfinal)
                    if np.isfinite(es) and es < best_exact_score:
                        best_exact_score = es
                        best_exact_seq = pfinal.copy()

    print(f"final exact check score = {best_exact_score:.8f}")
    return safe_final_sequence(best_exact_seq)
```
